# f931b55 Build gitkb knowledge notes covering all 53 commits after the history rewrite

## Summary
Adds the regenerated gitkb notes for every commit reachable from the rewritten history: 53 commit notes under knowledge/commits/ and 397 change notes under knowledge/changes/, named by the sha256 of the canonical commit-header-plus-diff text, plus two .gitkeep placeholders. The summaries were carried over from the notes written before the history rewrite, remapped to the new commit shas via git filter-repo's commit map, with the initial-commit entry edited so it no longer lists the removed ssl/ files.

## Why
git filter-repo changed every commit sha while purging ssl/key.pem and ssl/cert.pem, which made the previous notes (keyed by the old shas and hashes) unmatched; the knowledge directory was also dropped from history so only this clean set exists.

## Risks
The notes still describe a private key having once been committed in their Risks text for historical context, but no key material is present. Any change to the gitkb hash recipe would invalidate these file names again.

## Files
- `knowledge/changes/.gitkeep` (A, +0/-0): Empty placeholder that keeps the notes directory in version control. [note](../changes/2a28c5eb6105304709203203548f1b4954c7e07bec8243a36398e474676a0b21.md)
- `knowledge/changes/0003afc6224fc58b2aae0eb170bb4027ff67f084effafd266abf5cc5fb3178ff.md` (A, +38/-0): Generated gitkb change note for one file in one commit (front matter with path, status, line counts and change kind, then Summary and Notable symbols). [note](../changes/acb0557bfba5db9bf34ea200bfb3fc72c69945e1a8e11242e50faec7957daaf6.md)
- `knowledge/changes/0068caab4d66727310e20666b900878a6567133d91c80f701e95871e3b64e08d.md` (A, +34/-0): Generated gitkb change note for one file in one commit (front matter with path, status, line counts and change kind, then Summary and Notable symbols). [note](../changes/c195bad75364e4f9c0bb177bb24ad34c8909eb3d6817859a4c132404f55621cf.md)
- `knowledge/changes/0095fb8a533a7d81562c2a770ff43ea976415656e21f8abef3927a1dddd6a1a7.md` (A, +34/-0): Generated gitkb change note for one file in one commit (front matter with path, status, line counts and change kind, then Summary and Notable symbols).
